Dedicated 2 Maryam KWAIRANGA, Maman Hanan Allah yabar kauna dear, Allah kayara manah Hanan🙋🏻

    Yau takama ranar k'arshe dah su Ummulkhair suka gama papers aikuwa sunsha kala kalan parties and dinners,events kala kala harkasa attending dukkah sukayi, wasu suna murna wasu kuma basayi, Ummulkhair dah Ameesha sunanan a minne kamar superglue Ameesha tanata fad'a mata yanda zatayi missing d'inta, Ummulkhair mahakan dan Ummulkhair wannan shekara d'ayan dah sukayi tare hartayi adapting yanda Ameesha ke turanci itama haka takeyi dah tone d'in Indians Ameesha kuwa yanzu tah iya abincin hausawa iri iri nikam Nace zama dah mad'aukin kanwa, abun nasu sai mutum yaji babu dad'i idan yatuna rabuwa zasuyi, saura 1month najiran results dayake su en masters neh results d'insu baya dad'ewa sosai, haka sukayi wannan 1month d'in tamkar sati d'aya sun kar'bi result Alhamdulillah kowacce tafita dah 1st class kamar yanda dai suka saba daga Ameesha sai Ummulkhair, sunyi murna sosai dan basuda matsala lafiya klau sun rabu dah Maryam Abacha American university, 
1 Month later
gobe takama Saturday jirgin su Ummulkhair zai d'aga zuwa Nigeria yayinda itama Ameesha Insha Allah zata d'aga zuwa New Delhi, Ummulkhair tayi mata alk'awarin cewa idan har bikin tah yataso toh duk inda za ayi zataje mata, bah k'arramin murna Ameesha tayi dah hakan bah, kuma Ummulkhair tayi mata fad'a akan tarik'e musulunci kuma tah auri musulmi sabida taga familyn su Ameesha mix neh amman Ameesha tanada addini sosai shine yadad'a saka shak'uwar su tayi k'arfi sosai, haka suka rabu suna kuka har suka had'a inasu inasu Amrah ma tah tayasu kukan, 
 Gida Nigeria
   Abusaiyeed neh acikin mota yana driving ahankali, yasamu sauk'i bah laifi sabida yanda Hajiya Humaira tamishi alk'awarin nemo masa Ummulkhair indai tanah raye, hakan bah k'arramin kwantar mishi dah hankali yayi bah dan yasan Hajiya Humaira zata aikata, kwasam yanah tuki Ummulkhair tafad'o masa arai tuni yaji kamar abu ya soke shi azuciya haka yakama k'irjin shi yataka birki, sauran kad'an yahad'a babban accident, tuni titin yazama conjested kowa sai horn yakeyi kuka yafashe dashi yafito yahau kan bonnet d'in motar ya kwanta abinshi ya kanannade k'afa kamar yaro yanah kuka yanah fad'in "wayyo Allahnah Ummunah, Ummunah, Ummunah, Innalillahi kutaimaka kunemo min ita wayyo Ummunah", kash Innalillahi wa Inna ilaihi rajiun Antynmu yanzu koh Nijar ace tafi Nigeria yaushe rabon mushiga irin hold up d'innan amman Kinga Hold up lallai we are really back to Nigeria where gala dah fanta is ur only choice wen stock in hold up, dariya Ummulkhair tayi tace, galan dah fantan dai kikeso koh Amrah, kamar ina ganin abu neh yafaru kinga wancan fah akwance asaman mota sai wani rawan d'ari yake watak'il baida lafiya, lallai amman anyi d'an banza saikace gidan Ubansa, zomuje namishi magana, lallai Amrah dah k'arfin hali kike nidai banida wannan courage d'in kinmanta bah zuwa akayi d'aukan mu bah Cabman muka d'auko sabida kince muyiwa Ummanmu Surprise, sannan kid'auka zan biye miki ayaga min cert d'in shekara d'aya danayi ina karatu a Nijar, badani bah and U also u're going no where wlh, kina complain mai taxi ba dashi akeyi bah yaje gulma kekuma yanzu naga alaman Masscom kike karanta tunda gashi zakije d'aukan rahoto, dariya tah fashe dashi tah mik'awa Ummulkhair waya wanda Ameesha ke kiranta, dauka tayi cike dah farin ciki suka gaisa inda Ameesha take fad'a mata yanzu zasu tashi jirgin su zai d'aga nan Ummulkhair take bata labarin har sunzo Nigeria tama yimata videon datayi alk'awari harta turo on WhatsApp godiya Ameesha tamata sosai, nan Ummulkhair tace bara su k'arasa gida sai suyi waya hold up yayi yawa dahakan sukayi sallama,! Chab Antynmu gaskia keda Ameesha Kunasan junanku yanzu sabon layin dakika siya a airport harkin kirata dashi, wlh Saboda ita ma nasai layin tun a airport kuma ita kad'ai ce numban dan ita kad'ai nakira dashi,

Parlour kuwa tah koh ina fad'a akewa Abusaiyeed yagane mistakes d'inshi sosai mussaman dah Ummansu tamishi bayanin komai dalla dalla sosai ya tausaya wa Ummulkhair kuma yanemi yafiyar Iyayen tah, dana iyayen sah yayi musu alk'awarin hakan bazata k'ara faruwa bah, sosai sukayi musu Addu'a suka samusu Albarka, Mummy tah k'ara dacewa cikin daya b'are daman Allah yayi bazaizo duniya baneh Allah yakawo nah aike mai albarka, godiya yayi musu sosai yace Insha Allah dazaran yayi sallan magriba zai dinga komawa gida kafin Isha kuma yace no matter how busy he is bazai k'ara ignoring call d'inta bah danyaga hakan is part of their main problem, Addu'a aka yimusudah fatan Alkhairi, Alhaji Sufyan yabawa d'anshi kyautan mota sabuwa sabida shibai yardaba Hajiya Humaira tah wahalar mishi dah d'aa, haka suka wuni cikin farin ciki walwala dah raha, sai chan yamma aka kora Ummulkhair dah bulala dan cewa tayi babu inda zataje dah kukan tah kuwa suka bar gidan, ahanya kuwa Abusaiyeed banda dariya babu abinda yakeyi, gabad'aya abin yabashi dariya haushi taji tace, kuma wlh Malam babu ruwanka dani, opps sorry baby nifa badake nake bah, ni ina ruwana zama kayi bayani neh, street meat tagani saita tuno dah Nijar kullum aikin su kenan ita dah Ameesha Abusaiyeed tsaya tsaya tafad'a dah kwallan tah, kaga wancan tayi pointing naman, yanzu Ummunah sabida wannan kika sakani taka birkin dabanyi niyya bah kuma duk atsorace, toh nidai siya min yisauri nah matsu naci, fita yayi bah musu ya lodo mata dayawa yakawo matah Abusaiyeed ai yayi yawa nifa bazan iya cinyewa bah mesah kasiyo dayawa, ya salam Ummunah waike yanzu mita kika Koya neh koyaya Allah yashirya minke, toh Ameen ci tafara yi abinta koh damuwa dashi batayi bah suna isa yahad'a tah ita dah naman dukka yawuce dasu d'aki, ajiye tah yayi k'ura mishi ido tayi hey, why the stare? Uhm Allah Abusaiyeed karame dayawa gobe zanyi ma sakwara dah egusi soup kaci, dariya yayi yace, aikece ma sakwara if I hear wetin, awani gidan zakiyi sakwara kibari zance Ummanmu tayi manah, lallai Abusaiyeed wato Umman ce zatayi wahalan daka bah matar kah bah, "Exactly" ya amsa yanah rage kayan jikin shi, kah kyauta kaji, toh Ummunah kema haka yafad'a joining her on the bed, zip d'inta yazuge k'asa, fuska tah had'e tah zumburo baki kawai ina hutawa nah zaka fara koh, kai ya dungure matah yace, waike Ummunah ina tausayin ki yaje neh, dariya tayi tafad'a k'irjin shi tace, "k'irji nah mai dad'i nayi missing d'inshi" toh cire shi nabaki, A'a tafad'a dah k'arfi, mugu zaice nacire mushiga uku, dariya tayi kafin tagama yahad'e bakinsu, itama kissing d'inshi tafara yi daga nan abin yafi k'arfin Alk'alami nah naja k'ofa nafito,
1 Weak later 
  Rayuwar Ummulkhair dah Saiyadeen tah abin sha'awa har yanzu babu inda yaje harta gaji dah Koran shi sun saba kamar basu tab'a rabuwa bah, wani shak'uwa ce mai k'arfin gaske tashiga tsakanin su kamar shekarar su d'ari suna tare, komai tare sukeyi tun daga wanka har shafa mai, cin abinci, har kashi wani sa'in tare sukeyi koh tanah wanka yana kashi tayita toshe hanci shima kuma idan yazo turn d'inta tah rama, baya barin tah gashi bayaso yaganta cikin damuwa kona minti d'aya duk saiyabi ya rikice, har wajan ZeeSarki yasa takaishi yayi mata godiya had'i dah shopping nagani nafad'a, toh zee nidai lemon green bag d'innan kad'ai zaki bani😜, lols😂😆🤣 Happy Birthday In Advance sweetheart,  Washe gari Maman Hanan dah Baban Hanan dah sudais dah Matarshi agidan Su Ummulkhair suka wuni, babu uwa Maman Hanan tayi murnan dawowar Ummulkhair bakadan bah, har anfara jita~jitai a unguwa wai ankawo sabuwar Amarya kuma dah anje sai aga Ummulkhair ce haka maman hanan tadinga musu bayani wai tad'anyi tafiya neh, zeesarki Nakundu dah Biey cholli, Hassyluv teema dikko naganku, Ummulkhair tagode, rayuwa tah cigaba kullum kuwa Ummulkhair basa fasa waya dah Ameesha, yanzu dai Abusaiyeed yaji dad'i sosai Ummulkhair tanada knowledge akan health line sosai sabida masters d'in datayi, tah iya yin Allura koh wani iri tah iya in dripping mutum  kuma tah iya wankin ciwo tah iya dressing dah d'inki dah tests iri iri kai abubuwan dayawa suke wanda tasani, sosai yaji dad'in hakan kuma idan abu nadamun tah zata iya figuring menene on time, hakan bah k'arramin birge shi yayi bah dan ita dashi sunayin casual treatment agida kullum tanah tayashi aiki sabida yana dawowa dawuri, yanzu kuwa sai murna take koh jikin shi taji yayi warm saitace sai anyi Allura bashida lafiya ita adole tanah rama dukka alluran dayake mata abaya

2 Months Later
  Ummulkhair tagama examining kanta dakyau yau bayan Abusaiyeed yadawo wajajan k'arfe bakwai, tasame shi a parlour wak'a takunna tazauna akan ciyan shi bombom d'inta yana tab'a stick d'inshi tad'anyi Crazy dance sannan tace, "Baby I tink am pregnant" "are U're serious U're driving me crazy Ummunah yafad'a in a whisper" oh yes I have conceived ur baby, am glad to hear that am the most happiest Man on earth I can't wait to be a father to my unborn child, hmmm c'mon Abusaiyeed kanah damuna bansan hayaniya fah, am so weak wlh, ohh Ok am so sorry babynah, "u kno wat baby? Uhm saikin fad'a I dnt wanna have a big baby bump, everyone will know that am pregnant so how should we stop that, "No way baby" yafad'a yana rik'e fuskan tah tareda shafa cikin juz be careful as from now on I wouldn't want anything to happen to my baby kinji Ummunah, uhm naji kawo pregtrips muyi confirming there's no need mere looking at U ma nasan kinada ciki so dnt bother hakanma stress neh, tun aranar suka sanar dakowa tuni Hajiya Humaira tafara shirin shopping nah babies, Mummy dah Daddy kowa yayi murna Amrah kuwa kamar tayi tsalle daga Nijar har kuka tayi wai anyi babu itah, Ameesha ma ba abarta abaya bah dan saida tah turo dah videon rawan datayi nah murna, su ZeeSarki har ankawo packets d'in Pampers wai ana jiran zuwan baby, babu uwa Maman Hanan dayake tanah kayan bedsheets hartayi set d'in komai nah baby tun daga gado bags blankets sweaters babu abinda batayi bah uku uku, Baban Hanan kuwa zoben azurfa yasiyo nah jarirai guda biyu tunda itama Maman Hanan d'in abun yasamu, yace nah babyn su Ummulkhair d'aya nasu d'aya kai wannan tanadi haka Allah ubangiji yakawo su duniya lafiya, su Sudais ma dah Matar shi sun sha dawainiyya, en groups d'in Matar Dr Saiyadee dah Mhiss B~hal Novels dah Asalin rayuwa harsun fitar dah anko kai amman zumud'in ku yayi yawa, dan koyanzu kukaje wajan deelers d'innan zaku samu ankon ku, Maman Hanan, Maman Sadeeq Lagos, Maman Abudulnaeem, Maman Affan Deejah Nakundu,ZeeSarki, Miss Biey Cholli, Mama Sky, zee yellow, Subulluba, Rayana d bello, Binth Usman, My meena, My Sahla, My Asmeey, My Xiey Inuwa, My Asmeey Husnert, My Fateema dikko tahar abadan Insha Allah, Hassyluv k'awar arzik'i Allah yabarmin ke, dafatan zaki bani kyautan Ankon suna dan banida kud'i, toh wannan sunaye dana lissafo agurin anko saidai idan bakada kud'i domin indai dah kud'in ku toh daku za akwaso shoki, 
8 Months later
  Ummulkhair ce sai kuka take kamar baby dah alamun haihuwa zatayi tah rik'e hannun Abusaiyeed gum, cewa take nidai nasan mutuwa zanyi kah rik'e min babyn nan amana kada kah auro wacce zata wulakanta min duk abinda zan Haifa, habawa Abusaiyeed sai kuka shar shar kamar yaro Ummunah bazaki dena bah stop saying all this things those are awful words said out of pains, am here for U, I wunt leave U, gum yarik'e tah, daga k'arshe nurses suka wuce dah itah labour room, tuni yafashe dah kuka kamar yaro yana cewa Hajiya Humaira ai cewa tayi bazata fito dah rai bah, Hajiya Humaira kwantar mishi dah hankali tah dinga yi tanah cewa zafin labour neh it's common, Ummulkhair kuwa tayi kuka kamar ranta zai fita kuma sunan Abusaiyeed kad'ai take kira tanah cewa she loves him,sai Hajiya Humaira dah Mummy neh ketaya tah kiran Allah dah Annabi, haka tagama kukan tah sannan shuru yabiyo baya, nanfa hankali yatashi not until duk sukaji kukan baby Alhamdulillah Hajiya Humaira dah Mummy sukayi, Abusaiyeed kuwa banda ihu dah murna babu abinda yakeyi muryan Ummulkhair yaji tanah cewa, Saiyadeen Ummu I made it, I made it she's a baby girl, come have a look she looks like U, dasauri yashige ciki koh kimtsa Ummulkhair nurses basuyi bah, babyn kad'ai ya yarda sukayi wa wanka amman Ummulkhair shiya kimtsa tah tas tazama kamar bah ita tah haihu yanzu bah, Nurses neh suka mik'a mata babyntah aikuwa dakyar tah yarda Abusaiyeed yatab'a wai bazai d'auka bah ita kad'ai tayi labour bayan yace zai tayata, daga baya kuma tabashi video suka fara yiwa Ameesha suka tura mata tah WhatsApp, Inda Hajiya Humaira nan take tabawa Ummulkhair mukullin mota, she couldn't ask for more Ummulkhair tana yiwa d'anta kaunar dah batayi wa kanta, nan Mummy tace asaka sunan Hajiya Humaira sabida tayi dawainiyya dah Ummulkhair dayawa, nan take kuwa Hajiya Humaira tace sam daman tah riga tayanke shawara akan idan macece kuma merai toh Insha Allah Sunan ki za asaka Mummy dan haka Sunan Babyn nan Nafeesah Insha Allah, sai akira tah dah Ameesha murna fal cikin familyn nan ba amagana, Mummy tanah mamakin kirki irin nah Hajiya Humaira dan tah dad'e da fad'awa Mummy zata bawa Ummulkhair mota ranar data haihu koda kuwa babyn bai rayu bah, saikuma Allah yaraya abinsa, ansamu Ameesha tun daga asibiti fans d'inah suka cika su saiyadee dah kyauta wa enda sukayi nisa suka dinga transfer tah account dan harsun bud'ewa LilAmeesha Baby Account, tuni kud'i yacika shi kai Fans d'inah En Albarka iyayen kara, wanda basu kar'bi d'inki suna bah yanzu haka suna wajan tela sunabin layin d'inki, wasu harsun fara kitso dah kunshi zu Xiey Inuwa dah Asmee Husnert bah amagana k'irjin suna, suna gidan kitso ana fama dah sauro, kai Maman Hanan er Albarka tunda akayi wannan haihuwar tanah asibiti gashi itama abin tausayi duk tayi nauyi, amman saida taje har aka sallame su Ummulkhair haka tadinga yiwa Baban Hanan magiya harya Barta takwana gidan su Ummulkhair tabarshi dah renon Hanan, yanzu duk inda kah lek'a fah adangin Ummulkhair dah Saiyadee shirye shirye suna akeyi, sai buhunhunan rengen aketa saukewa, AntyMuna ce take kula dah Ummulkhair wajan sit bath dan agida suka tarar dah itah dah aka sallamo su daga Asibiti, yanzu shiri sukeyi sosai dah Abusaiyeed suyita tsokanan junansu, tanajin dad'in yanda yake kula dah Ummulkhair sosai abin yanah birge tah taga yanda yayi adjusting schedules d'insa gabad'aya, godiya tayiwa Allah dan tasan hakan yana nufin mai saukin kaine Abusaiyeed kawai cax akwai dubu dabazu iyayin hakan bah saidai idan mace tah hak'ura dah zaman dasu kawai! 3 Months Later
  Ummulkhair ce zaune duk tarasa sukuni sai kuka takeyi dan kuwa LilAmeesha bah lafiya, kuma ita yanzu tashin hankalin tah bazata iya jurewa bah ayi mata Allura hakan yasa yau kwana d'aya kenan take avoiding Abusaiyeed, yauma kamar kullum yadawo yakula tanah avoiding d'inshi, kuka yafara yana Neman yafiyan tah dan gani yake yamata lefi, Ummunah kiyiwa Allah ki tausaya wa rayuwa tah idan wani abun namiki kifad'a min, wlh kin jefa ni cikin wani hali ni bazan iya rayuwa tah babu keba, kece farin ciki nah dake dah Ameeshan mu, pls kitausaya min, Ajiye Ameesha tayi taje tah rungume shi sannan tace, "ni babu abinda kayimin banasan katab'a min Ameesha nah neh, kallon tah yake cike dah mamaki sannan yace yau kuma Ummunah? Uhm nidai banso but Ummunah I miss her so much tun jiya fah bamu gaisa bah, yau d'inma bazaki bari bah, yes tah amsa tah d'auke kai, toh shikenan naji Ummunah, but pls dnt make it long I miss her, Ok tace sannan tah rungume shi wata duniyar suka fad'a kafin daga bisani tah tashi dah niyyar d'auko wayan tah dake ringing, Ameesha dake kwance akan kujera neh tanah kukan wahala, kallon kauna Abusaiyeed yake mata yanaso yayi saurin dubata amman yana tsoron Ummu tazo takama shi hakan yasa yake kallon tah daga nesa not until sanda tah kusan fad'owa daga kan kujera baisan sanda yayi tsalle bah ya cafko tah, wani razanannen kuka tasaka jikin tah yaji da zafi sosai koh ba afad'a masa bah yasan ershi is suffering from fever, dasauri yafara kiran Ummulkhair wanda itama tun sanda taji kukan Ameesha tafito, tanah ganin Ameesha ahannun Abusaiyeed tafashe dah kuka tace, "shikenan yanzu kasan batada lafiya zakayi mata Allura, wayyo Allahnah kutaimake ni shiga uku" Abusaiyeed tsayawa yayi baki awangale yanah kallon Ummulkhair yanda take kuka tsakanin tah dah Allah, mamakin shi kuwa yacce tah b'oye rashin lafiyar Ameesha sabida kada amata Allura, dakyar bakin shi yanah rawa ya iya cewa, "Ummunah do U call this love, if U do then pls stop it, mesah why on earth zaki b'oye min she's sick, she's our baby for Allah's sake her health matters alot, why would U hide such a thing, yanzu idan nayi tafiya bazaki yi treating d'inta bah, ai idanma bazaki iyaba saiki kaita wani wajen ayi treating d'inta shine kaunar dazaki nuna mata, shuru batace komai bah sai kuka dah takeyi kamar anmata duka ahankali yayi placing Ameesha ak'irjin shi yashiga had'a wasu Allurai batare dah b'ata lokaci bah yagama had'awa, kwantar dah ita yayi tah baya yacire mata diapers yarinyar har wani hakk'i take nah azaba, tun kafin yasoka Ummulkhair tafara ihu tanah kuka tanah fad'in wlh dazafi nidai kabarmin babynah babu ruwanka damu batasan Allura fah ita Ameesha, kallon tah yayi yace, "zakimin shuru koh sai nah mauje ki" a hankali yafara injecting Ameesha amazaunin tah, Ummulkhair tsalle tafara yi tanah kuka harda yarfe hannu wai wayyo Allah yana mata zafi, ita yarinyar ma dan tsananin azaba batayi kuka bah amman Ummulkhair kuka take tsakani dah Allah, bayan yagama yamaida matah dah diapers yace bara nabaki magani kibata, hannun tah biyu tasa tanata dukkan Abusaiyeed ak'irjin sah wai yayiwa babyn ta  Allura, saida tabige shi iya son ranta sannan tah rungume shi tanah kuka, toh Ummunah wai daman bakyasan tasamu sauk'i shikenan tunda hakane, babyn tah tad'auka tace kumama bah ruwanka damu tawuce taje tayi kwanciyar tah, hakannan saita fara bawa little Ameesha hak'uri wai adole batada lafiya anyi mata Allura koh tab'a er bata bari yayi, maganin ma kin bata tayi tasha, hakan yasa yafita harkan tah, dasafe wasa wasa Ummulkhair tagama abinci tah amman sam Abusaiyeed beko kalli wajan bah yayiwa Ameesha Allura yafice hakan bah k'arramin b'ata mata rai yayi bah, dan haka kafin yadawo tasa aka buga mata wani d'an k'arramin cage sannan tashiga gun Maman Hanan tah kwashe yaran mage guda uku tah aika gidan su akawo mata muzurun tah wanda takeso amman tah hak'ura tabar shi agida sabida Abusaiyeed bayason mage koh kad'an aikuwa duk tasa Affan yakawo mata Babban Muzuru tah, Tuni tah ajiye su a cage, daddare bayan Abusaiyeed yadawo taga nanma bai kulata bah sai Ameesha daya d'auka yana wasa dah itah, Ummulkhair Ce tah tsaya agaban shi tanah kallon shi, kafin tace, "wai dagaske bazaka kulani bah" kallon tah yake yaji duk yayi missing d'inta dayawa, yanzu babu abinda yakeso sai yajita ajikin sa, ajiye Ameesha yayi yace, "taho toh" wlh bazan zo bah d'in saida kawani gama jamin aji ank'i azo d'in, toh Ummunah ba kece bah baki damu dah Babynmu bah batada lafiya kinbarta cikin wahala kodan batada bakin gaya miki halin dah take ciki, toh nidai kada kasake yimata allura, duk wajan alluran ciwo yake mata, "tace miki hakan?" Aiba saita fad'a bah, alluran ma dazafi gareshi, toh sorry zo kiyi breastfeeding d'inta sainayi mata Allura, a'a batajin yunwa, Ummunah siyan nonon kike dah bazaki bata bah, lokacin alluran tah fah yayi, nidai  Abusaiyeed bah ruwanka damu, Next on MDS @Pg. 70
